# Complete Old-to-New Course Coverage Map (22 Chapters → 10 Sections, 111 Lessons, 1,361 Checkpoints)

- **Authoritative Source:** `session_viewer/src`
- **Main Sections:** 10
- **Absorbed Chapters:** 22
- **Reader-Facing Consolidated Lessons:** 111
- **Preserved Coding Checkpoints:** 1,360 (`01-canvas` .. `96zzzzzzzq-draw-original-cad-wire-curves`)
- **Preserved Reference Comparison:** 1 (`97-finish-with-the-maintained-browser-runtime`)
- **Total Preserved Checkpoint IDs:** 1,361
- **Recovery Path for Any Checkpoint `<ID>`:**
  - Install dependencies/assets: `npm --prefix ../session_tests run course -- dependencies <ID>`
  - Verify typed source: `npm --prefix ../session_tests run course -- check <ID>`
  - Save checkpoint: `npm --prefix ../session_tests run course -- save <ID>`
  - Restore saved checkpoint: `npm --prefix ../session_tests run course -- restore <ID>`
  - Recovery guide: [`docs/journey/recovery.md`](../recovery.md)
